# Changes and decisions

Counts are loci, not words or error-rate estimates. Changes include resolved or revised doubt markings; pure macro translations are excluded. A locus may differ from both witnesses. Confidence columns count distinct loci. Retained C doubts are separately documented even where the reading is unchanged.

## Consequential reading corrections

- 97/019: Against H: the codim singleton has no overbar; the separate cycle singleton is barred.
- 97/019: Against H: the proposition uses a wavy leadsto arrow. The summation has a detached stroke retained with doubt; its role is not silently supplied.
- 123/002: The source has il faut et suffit in a); C’s extra il is removed.
- 123/002: H omits the live middle Soit D une in the bottom three-layer correction; it is restored.
- 123/002: The extension question’s square closing bracket, the first-occurrence centralizer alphabet doubt, and the physical circled-x/tensor ambiguity are retained; c^h is not emended to the mathematically expected c^n.
- 123/003: D overtypes W in normalisateur de: the overtyped earlier W is retained instead of treating the glyph as double-struck D.
- 123/003: H omits the canceled de under primitive and loses the interlinear status of the commuting-action clause; both are retained.
- 123/016: 123/016 b): the visible g(-2) target is retained; Jacobi and an explicit sl2 matrix computation expose the sign problem without emending it.
- 123/016: 123/016 (ii): H’s e≠0 is rejected for the visible e=0; the separate slash remains at i.e., and the exp(0)=1 contradiction is recorded only in the notes.
- 123/016: 123/016 d): two visible prime-like strokes on the second f remain uncertain; no differential convention is imposed.
- 123/017: 123/017: the live a+g^e survives the correction to Lie(Z+); its explicit SL₂×SL₂ counterexample is recorded separately, without emending the source.
- 123/017: 123/017: the corrected Lie U lower bound 1 is resolved from the overprinted digit, with old 0 preserved.
- 123/017: 123/017: the marginal relation is ≃, and the written fraction before -direct replaces both witnesses’ expansion semi-direct.
- 123/018: 123/018: Gamma-prime is resolved to Gamma by its connected terminal; the three filtration symbols are resolved as underlined slashed ovals.
- 123/018: 123/018: ou encore belongs after P in the explanatory parenthesis, rather than in the preceding class sentence as H places it.
- 123/018: 123/018: the stabilizer’s semi-simplicity is an authorial question; an explicit SL3 example gives a one-dimensional torus. The cancelled Isom-torsor claim fails in the separately checked product example.
- 123/019: The second opening map is resolved as mapsto; all three filtration glyphs are underlined slashed ovals. Typed o and handwritten o/0 are distinguished locally.
- 123/019: The condition says que G est; the cancelled phrase is les/des i rather than ceci. The equivalence insertion ends with a barred m, not a fully written même.
- 123/019: The A₃ marginal clause remains literal with its antecedent unresolved. The explicit weight examples refute only an injectivity interpretation of that clause, not every possible reference of the note.
- 123/020: Both mathematical intervals remain physically blank: no Lie-algebra letter before (1)=0 and no arrow between Sl(2,k) and G.
- 123/020: The interior Fil mark is preserved with point/comma doubt and the typed index is resolved as Z_o.
- 123/020: The conditional g(1)=0 criterion and the unqualified unit-stabilizer statement are discussed separately in the notes; their counterexamples never emend the source.
- 123/022: The body has underlined plain Z letters and crossed-oval phi glyphs; the footer’s integer alphabet and open varphi are read separately.
- 123/022: The first exponent 2 is secure, its medium is not; the footer’s second denominator Z retains a local plain/double-struck doubt.
- 123/022: Both insertion curves and the lower-row footer geometry are restored; no solidus grouping or unit-group star placement is mathematically repaired.
- 123/023: The letter literally expects rank < r-1 in the other root-length cases. The crop confirms the strict <; an internal C2/PSp4 calculation refutes that universal expectation without changing the source.
- 123/023: Underlined ordinary g, h and O are preserved; neither Fraktur nor script is substituted. The cancelled O-adjacent t/star remains local and has no assigned mathematical role.
- 123/023: The raised n- is certain and yields n-r-2 with the baseline r-2; its medium is uncertain. Both leaders, cancellation layers and unresolved subscript overlays remain.
- 123/028: The typed ordinary underlined U in the adjoint criterion is preserved; H’s script U is unsupported by the ink.
- 123/028: The parenthetical E has uncertain extra lower material, not a resolved second E impression; no definite blackboard E is supplied.
- 123/028: The finite-projective-limit phrase remains locally live after the raised exactness insertion; H’s replacement interpretation is rejected.
- 123/028: The adjoint criterion was checked only under explicit universe and topos structural hypotheses; none of the unseen numbered references was verified.
- 123/040: The top note reads fonctorielles, not H’s fonctionnelles.
- 123/040: The typed est présenced’, with raised pen en, belongs to section 3.1.3; H misattaches en présence d’ to the preceding handwritten line.
- 123/040: The footer begins en un foncteur, not H’s ou un foncteur.
- 123/040: The f:E→F and u:F→E directions, the upper/lower stars, and the ordinary underlined U are retained from the ink. H’s synopsis alphabet is not counted as a direct glyph error.
- 123/040: The fixed-point examples substantiate failures of epimorphism, torsor, free-module and tensor preservation; the bialgebra example concerns the inherited comultiplication only. No mathematical correction is inserted into the source.
- 162-1/003: Calcul: an independent opening parenthesis after K and a separate P with raised n are resolved, eliminating C’s CP alternative. The inner L/bar remains locally doubtful (D037–D038).
- 162-1/003: The Serre title’s =0 and the full K-expression, left unread in H, are retained from their visible strokes. This restores mathematical content but does not verify either expression (D035–D038, ROOT-D104).
